# Every question pattern: CBSE Class 10 Maths (Standard)

Generated by `scripts/qb-report.py`; do not edit by hand. One row is one distinct kind of question in one slot.

- **Target** and **Have** read easy / medium / hard.
- **Fig**: needs a printed figure. **App**: application or higher-order.
- **Asked in**: the board papers where this kind of question appeared, from the 149 Standard papers tagged (2011-2026, every set downloaded; main, compartment and second exams; each question linked to one pattern). Sources: PYQ_SOURCES.md. A blank means syllabus-only so far.
- **Sample**: an illustration of the pattern, not a bank question. Samples have not gone through the full verification checks.
- *(verify)* topics are waiting on the official syllabus.

### Decimal expansions and number classification

> Holds only classification tasks, which 2023-2026 papers test. Terminating-decimal rules are in a separate verify topic.

### Division algorithm and evaluation *(verify)*

> The polynomial division algorithm is on the believed-cut list. Every division row on the board is legacy (2018, 2019); the one 2024 row tagged division actually tests the degree of the zero polynomial, so it sits under degree patterns. Targets 0 unless a person confirms the syllabus. Evaluation is kept as a core pattern under Degree, terms and value.

### Combination figures (shaded regions) *(verify)*

> The combination-of-plane-figures section is believed cut, but recent papers still test it: 2024 main has horses at the four corners of a square, 2025 main has a square house inside a circular plot, and 2025 compartment and 2026 second both have shaded-area diagrams. So B, E and MCQ patterns carry real targets. C and D patterns have target 0: their board evidence is 2016-2019 only, and core patterns fill those slots. A person must confirm the syllabus; if the section is out, remove this topic and rebalance.
